∑_{ij} X_{ij} = 1.

Pseudo-gradient algorithm details:
Input p normalized length n.
State z=log(p)-mean.
Adam m,v.
For each iter:
  c=fft_convolve(p)
  Choose t and beta.
  weights w = softmax((c - t)/beta) maybe if using target. Or if standard, softmax((c - max)/beta).
  g_p = corr(w,p) (without 2). dot=p.g_p.
  g_z = p*(g_p - dot).
  Adam update z -= lr * ...
  p=softmax(z)
  score exact 2n max c/psum².
Need efficient: c FFT each iter. For n=4096, N=8192. rfft 8192 ~0.0002s? In Python maybe 0.001. iterations 10000 ~10s. Good.

Smooth max weights: use beta. If beta small, w spike; gradient may be unstable. Use adaptive beta based on score gap to target. Maybe use a schedule:
- Initially beta = 0.05 * maxc (or 1/n scale). Since c max ~ score/(2n). For n=4096 score1.5, maxc~1.8e-4. beta ~1e-5. Not too small.
- Decrease beta when progress stalls.
- Use target t: start t = maxc; decrease t by max(1e-5, 0.005*maxc) each successful block? But if t below feasible, gradient tries to push all below t, may increase some other peaks. We can alternate t near current max.

A robust scheme: Use smooth max of transformed values `(c - alpha) / beta` with alpha = (1 - ε)*current_max? For α close to max, weights only on peaks exceeding α. This is like focusing violations. If α too low, no weights. Set α = (1 - δ)*maxc, δ ~0.02. Weights on near-max. Then after many iterations, max may drop; α follows.

Alternatively, use `w = softmax((c - maxc)/beta)`; weights include all but peak. Fine.

Adam on logit may be slow near optimum. Add "coordinate mass moves" to fine-tune.

Mass-move local search improvement:
For current p, compute c. For a small step ε, moving mass from i to j changes c linearly. We can select i as positions contributing to peak lags (high heat) and j as cold positions. Previous peak flooding does this multiplicatively. We can make it vectorized and adaptive.

Heat computation: For lag k, contribution to c_k from position i is p_i p_{k-i}. We want reduce high lags by lowering p_i if it pairs with many high lags. Define heat_i =∑_k w_k p_{k-i} (same as gradient g_i before p factor). This is exactly correlation w_p. It indicates how increasing p_i would increase weighted smooth objective. To reduce peaks, we should decrease p_i with high heat and increase low heat. Multiplicative update p_i *= exp(-η * (heat_i - mean?)) normalized is like mirror gradient. This may be more stable than Adam. We can implement dynamical system:
  z_i -= η * (heat_i - mean(heat)) perhaps; p=softmax(z). This is plain gradient on logits without Adam. With small η.
  Use line search/backtrack to ensure score decreases. Since objective nonconvex, but local.

We can combine: Adam for many dims, then mass-shuffling:
- For current p, compute heat=h (n). Sort low heat and high heat. Move a fraction δ from high heat to low heat, respecting nonneg. Evaluate score; accept if improves. This is coordinate descent. We can vectorize moving a batch: choose top H high and bottom H low, small ε. Since each move affects O(n) conv coefficients; evaluating full FFT each time okay for maybe hundreds.
- To pick j, choose low heat positions not too close to partner? Heat captures all lags.
- This can escape local plateaus.

Previous peak_flood uses heat from hot lags and multiplicative update. We can improve by using smooth weights w from softmax (not threshold) and exponential update.

Let's design `grad_descent_logit` function with options:
- p, deadline, rng, lr, beta_frac, beta_decay, no_imp_limit, use_target.
- At restart, add noise scaled by best score.
- Use adaptive learning rate: if score improved, maintain/increase slightly; if no improvement, decay.
- Use trigonometric annealing? maintain beta between max(1e-6/n?) and beta_frac*maxc.
- Important: Since p sum=1, adding constant to z no effect; subtract mean after update.

For target 1.5, max/min maybe? If density f min near 0? Could have zeros? Nonnegative; values may have orders. But logp range maybe up to ~log n? def mass_moves(a, deadline, n_moves=200, batch=64):
```
   a normalized, best=score

     c=conv(a); maxc=c.max(); heat=correlation(softmax(c,beta),a)

     total_eps = η * sum(a[hot]) ; move = total_eps * a[hot]/sum? distribute to cold maybe equal or opposite heat.

```

```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, low_amp=0.2, high_amp=0.02, max_freq=64):
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,min(max_freq,n//2)):
      amp = low_amp * (k/max_freq)**2 + high_amp
      phase=2π*rng.random()
      noise += amp*(np.cos(2πk*np.arange(n)/n)+rng.random()*np.sin?)  # real
   return noise
```

```
def adam_opt(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, beta_frac=0.1, beta_decay=0.95, no_imp_limit=300, noise_scale=None, base_n=1024):
  p = norm_nonneg(p0); n=p.size
  z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
  b1,b2,eps=0.9,0.999,1e-8
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
  maxc=conv max; beta=beta_frac*maxc
  best=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); best_score=score; no=0; focus=1.0; current_lr=lr
  while time<deadline:
     c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=c.max(); if not finite... 
     w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus)
     g_p=corr_w_p(w,p)
     dot=float(np.dot(p,g_p))
     gz=p*(g_p-dot)
     gz-=gz.mean()
     if not finite: z=best_z; m=v=0; t=0; continue
     t+=1; m=...
     z = z - current_lr * mh/(sqrt(vh)+eps)
     z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=z.mean()
     p=softmax_from_z(z)
     c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=c.max(); score=2*n*maxc (sum1)
     if score < best_score - 1e-14:
        best...
        no=0; focus_rules...
     else:
        no+=1;
        if no%50==0: current_lr=max(0.002, current_lr*0.97)
        if no%200==0: focus=min(3.0, focus*1.2)
     if t%20==0:
        beta=max(1e-8, beta*beta_decay)
        # adapt beta to score gap maybe
        gap = maxc - best_score/(2*n)
        beta_target = max(1e-8, min(beta_frac*maxc, 0.1*gap if gap>0 else 1e-8))? 
     restart if no>limit:
        z=best_z + noise (smooth or iid)
        p=softmax; beta=...; m=v=0; current_lr=lr; focus=1; no=0; t=0
  return best, best_score
```

```
def mass_moves(p0, deadline, rng, batch=32, eta=0.05, n_trials=0):
  p=norm; best=p; best_score=score; z=np.log(p)-mean
  while time<deadline:
     c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=c.max(); score=...
     if score<best...
     beta=max(1e-8, 0.05*maxc)
     w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus=2) # focus peaks
     hp=corr_w_p(w,p)
     # heat normalized
     hot=np.argsort(hp)[::-1][:batch]
     cold=np.argsort(hp)[:batch]
     # ensure some cold not same
     # move fractions
     deltas = eta * p[hot]
     # distribute to cold in order; maybe random shuffle cold
     for idx_h in hot:
        idx_c = cold[rng.integers(batch)]
        d = eta*p[idx_h]
        p[idx_h]-=d; p[idx_c]+=d
     p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
     new_score=score_seq(p)
     if new_score < best_score - 1e-15 or (new_score < current_score - 1e-15): accept
     else: revert to previous accepted, maybe reduce eta
     eta = max(0.005, eta*0.98 if no improve)
```

`lp_solve` top-level:
```
def lp_solve(a, M, time_limit=60.0):
   n=a.size
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a=a/a.sum()
   rows=np.empty(n*(n+1)//2, dtype=np.int32)
   cols=np.empty(..., dtype=np.int32)
   data=np.empty(..., dtype=np.float64)
   pos=0
   for d in range(n):
      cnt=n-d
      rows[pos:pos+cnt]=np.arange(d,d+cnt,dtype=np.int32)
      cols[pos:pos+cnt]=np.arange(cnt,dtype=np.int32)
      data[pos:pos+cnt]=a[d]
      pos+=cnt
   A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
   c_obj=-np.ones(n)
   b_ub=M*np.ones(2*n-1)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
   res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': max(1.0,float(time_limit))})
   if res is not None and res.success:
      return np.maximum(res.x,0.0)
   return None
```
Could `options={'time_limit':...}` not supported in old SciPy? It is supported for HiGHS. If error, fallback without options. Use try.
If time_limit >100, still okay. But building arrays inside each solve repeated; we can prebuild A once per `lp_refine` call for given a? A changes with a each iteration. Could solve multiple M with same A? a changes after mix. But if we only solve once per a, build once. In lp_refine loop, a changes. For multiple M attempts for same a, build A once. We'll make `lp_solve` accept prebuilt A? Or in ref refine build A for current a and call linprog multiple M.
```
def build_conv_matrix(a): ...
def lp_solve_prebuilt(A,c_obj,b_ub,bounds,time_limit): ...
```
Top-level. Good.

LP refine:
```
def lp_refine(a0, deadline, max_solves=10, t=0.35, shrink=0.998):
  a=norm; best=a; best_score=score
  n=a.size
  for solve in range(max_solves):
    if time>deadline-(15 if n>1024 else 5): break
    A=build_conv_matrix(a)
    c=np.full(2*n-1, M)
    # try Ms: current_max*shrink, maybe current_max*0.99, current_max
    b=None
    for factor in [shrink, 0.998, 1.0]:
       b=lp_solve_prebuilt(A,... M*f, time_limit=...)
       if b is not None: break
    if b is None: break
    sb=b.sum(); if sb<1e-8: break
    b=b/sb
    a_new=(1-t)*a+t*b; a_new=norm
    sc=score
    if sc<best_score: best...
    if sc < best_score*1.03:
       a=a_new
    else:
       a=best.copy()
    M=max(float(conv(a).max())*shrink, best_score/(2*n)*1.005)
    t=max(0.1,t*0.95)
  return best,best_score
```
But if first factor shrink infeasible, 0.998 maybe, 1.0 feasible. We should try slightly lower to push. Good.

Now, mass moves top-level:
```
def mass_moves(a0, deadline, rng, batch=16, max_iters=1000):
  a=norm; best=a.copy(); best_score=score; current_score=best_score
  eta=0.05; no=0
  while time<deadline:
    c=fft_convolve(a); maxc=c.max(); score=2*n*maxc
    if score<best...
    beta=max(1e-12, 0.05*maxc)
    w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus=2.0)
    hp=corr_w_p(w,a)
    # select hot/cold
    hot=np.argsort(hp)[::-1]
    cold=np.argsort(hp)
    # random sample pairs
    improved=False
    for trial in range(4):
       i=hot[rng.integers(min(32,hot.size))]
       j=cold[rng.integers(min(32,cold.size))]
       if i==j: continue
       for frac in (0.01,0.03,0.08):
           cand=a.copy()
           d=frac*min(cand[i], 1.0/n) # not too much
           cand[i]-=d; cand[j]+=d
           cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-300); cand/=cand.sum()
           sc=score_seq(cand)
           if sc<best_score:
               best=cand; best_score=sc; a=cand; current_score=sc; improved=True; break
           if sc<current_score - 1e-16:
               a=cand; current_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if improved: break
    if not improved:
       # multiplicative small step using hp
       z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
       step=eta*(hp - hp.mean())
       z=z - 0.02*step
       cand=softmax_from_z(z)
       sc=score_seq(cand)
       if sc<current_score:
          a=cand; current_score=sc
       else:
          eta=max(0.002,eta*0.9)
          if no>200: restart with smooth noise around best
    else: eta=min(0.2, eta*1.05); no=0
    if not improved: no+=1
  return best,best_score
```
This may be slow due copying and FFT each trial. Limit. For n=4096, 4 trials*3 fracs*FFT ~12 FFT per iter; 100 iters=1200 FFT, okay maybe 6s. But if no improvement, one extra. We can reduce: just do a single batch multiplicative update and accept if better; less overhead. Maybe simpler and robust.

```
def heat_mirror_opt(p0, deadline, rng, eta=0.05, beta_frac=0.05, focus=1.5):
   z=log(p); eta current
   for it:
      c,w,hp
      # symmetrize update in log space: delta = eta * (hp - mean(hp)) / (max(abs)? or n?)
      delta = eta*(hp - hp.mean())
      # scale delta to O(eta): hp magnitude? divide by (2*max(abs(hp)) or 1)
      delta = delta / max(1.0, np.max(np.abs(hp)))

      if improve eta*=1.05 else eta*=0.9; if no improve restart noise around best
```

```
def make_basis(n, K=6):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   cols=[np.ones_like(x), x, x*x]
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x))
      cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x))
   return np.column_stack(cols)

def profile_from_basis(theta, basis):
   z = basis @ theta
   z = np.clip(z, -5, 5)
   p = np.exp(z); p/=p.sum(); return p
```

Pseudo-code top-level:
```
def adam_opt(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, beta_frac=0.08, beta_decay=0.95, no_imp_limit=250, noise_scale=None, focus_start=1.0):
    p = normalize_nonneg(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n = p.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)); z -= z.mean()
    b1,b2,eps=0.9,0.999,1e-8
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=max(c); beta=max(1e-12, beta_frac*maxc)
    best=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); best_score=2*n*maxc
    no=0; focus=focus_start; current_lr=lr
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=fft_convolve(p)
        if c.size==0: break
        maxc=float(c.max())
        w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus)
        gp=corr_w_p(w,p)
        dot=float(np.dot(p,gp))
        gz=p*(gp-dot)
        gz-=gz.mean()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
            z=best_z.copy(); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; continue
        t+=1
        m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gz
        v=b2*v+(1-b2)*(gz*gz)
        mh=m/(1-b1**t)
        vh=v/(1-b2**t)
        z = z - current_lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            z=best_z.copy(); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; continue
        z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=z.mean()
        p=softmax_from_z(z)
        c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=float(c.max()); score=float(2.0*n*maxc)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score=score; best=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); no=0
                focus=Math? if no previously: focus=max(focus_start, focus*0.8)
                current_lr=min(0.2, current_lr*1.01)
            else:
                no+=1
                if no % 30 == 0:
                    current_lr=max(0.002, current_lr*0.97)
                if no % 100 == 0:
                    focus=min(4.0, focus*1.15)
            # beta adapt
            if t % 10 == 0:
                # target beta based on best gap? use maxc
                beta = max(1e-10, beta * beta_decay)
                # if close to best, reduce less? 
        else: ...
        if no > no_imp_limit:
            # restart
            ns = noise_scale if not None else (0.05 if best_score <1.51 else 0.15)
            if rng is not None:
                nz = smooth_noise(n, rng, ns, 32)
            else:
                nz = 0
            z = best_z + nz
            z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=z.mean()
            p=softmax_from_z(z)
            beta=max(1e-6, beta_frac*(2*best_score/n))
            m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; focus=focus_start; no=0
    return best, best_score
```
Need avoid `Math` typo. Use `focus = max(focus_start, focus*0.85)`.

```
def make_best_interp(bases, n_target):
    best_p=None; best_s=np.inf
    seen=set()
    for b in bases:
       if b is None: continue
       key=b.size
       # for len==target, p=b
       for mode in (0,1):
          if b.size==n_target: p=b if mode==0 else b
          else:
             if mode==0: p=interp_profile(b,n_target)
             else: p=repeat_profile(b,n_target)
          if p is None: continue
          s=score_seq(p)
          if s<best_s: best_p=p.copy(); best_s=s
    return best_p,best_s
```

Detailed functions:

`normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-12: return None
    return a/s
```
For LP floor maybe separate.

`fft_convolve(a)`:
```
def fft_convolve(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a.astype(np.float64), N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[c<0]=0.0
    return c
```
Could negative due rounding; max.

`score_seq(a)`:
```
def score_seq(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   if a is None: return np.inf
   c=fft_convolve(a)
   if c.size==0: return np.inf
   maxc=float(c.max())
   s=float(a.sum())
   return float(2.0*a.size*maxc/(s*s))
```
Since a normalized, s=1, but keep.

`softmax_from_z(z)`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size==0: return None
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
   z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
   z=z-z.max()
   e=np.exp(z)
   e=e+1e-300
   s=float(e.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
   return e/s
```
Floor e-300, sum maybe n*e-300, okay.

`smooth_noise(n, rng, amp=0.2, max_freq=32)`:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, amp=0.2, max_freq=32):
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    m=min(max_freq, n//2)
    for k in range(1,m+1):
       a=amp/float(k)
       phase=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
       noise += a * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*x/n + phase)
    noise -= noise.mean()
    mx=float(np.max(np.abs(noise))) if noise.size else 0.0
    if mx>0 and np.isfinite(mx): noise *= (amp/mx)  # final amp? This double scales. Maybe just return noise if amp already. 
    return noise
```
If we multiply by amp again, magnitude ~amp. But noise sum amplitudes ~amp H_m ~amp log, times amp/mx => amp. Good. If amp=0.1, noise ~0.1. If amp=0.5, ~0.5.
Maybe use `noise *= amp / mx` but if mx already includes amp, result amplitude amp, okay.

`soft_weights(c, beta, focus=1.0)`:
```
def soft_weights(c, beta, focus=1.0):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
    m=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): m=0.0; c=np.zeros_like(c)
    if beta <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        w=np.zeros(c.size)
        mask=c >= m - 1e-12
        cnt=int(mask.sum())
        if cnt==0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
        else: w[mask]=1.0/cnt
        return w
    x=(c-m)/beta
    # x <=0
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w
    w=ex/s
    if focus != 1.0:
       focus=float(focus)
       if focus>1.0:
          w=np.power(w, focus)
          sw=float(w.sum())
          if sw>0 and np.isfinite(sw): w/=sw
          else: w=np.zeros_like; w[argmax]=1
    return w
```
`np.power(w, focus)` for focus maybe 2-4; okay.

`corr_w_p(w, p)`:
```
def corr_w_p(w,p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel(); w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=p.size
   if w.size != 2*n-1:
       # if mismatch, truncate/pad? Try adjust
       if w.size < 2*n-1:
          tmp=np.zeros(2*n-1); tmp[:w.size]=w[:min(w.size,2*n-1)]; w=tmp
       else: w=w[:2*n-1]
   N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fw*fp,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
   h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
   return h
```
Use N=2n-1. For n=1, length1, N=1, slice [0:1] okay.

`adam_opt` implementation with exact local score. Need careful with time. Use `start_time`? No.

```
def adam_opt(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, beta_frac=0.08, beta_decay=0.95, no_imp_limit=250, noise_amp=None):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p0)
    if p is None: return None,np.inf
    n=p.size
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    b1,b2,eps=0.9,0.999,1e-8
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    beta=max(1e-12, beta_frac*maxc)
    best_score=float(2.0*n*maxc)
    best=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy()
    no=0; focus=1.0; current_lr=lr
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=fft_convolve(p)
       if c.size==0: break
       maxc=float(c.max())
       w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus)
       gp=corr_w_p(w,p)
       dot=float(np.dot(p,gp))
       gz=p*(gp-dot)
       gz-=gz.mean()
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
           z=best_z.copy(); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; continue
       t+=1
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gz
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*(gz*gz)
       mh=m/(1-b1**t)
       vh=v/(1-b2**t)
       z = z - current_lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
           z=best_z.copy(); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; continue
       z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=z.mean()
       p=softmax_from_z(z)
       c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
       score=float(2.0*n*maxc)
       if np.isfinite(score):
          if score < best_score - 1e-14:
              best_score=score; best=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); no=0
              focus=max(1.0, focus*0.85)
              current_lr=min(0.2, current_lr*1.02)
          else:
              no+=1
              if no % 30 ==0: current_lr=max(0.002, current_lr*0.97)
              if no % 100 ==0: focus=min(4.0, focus*1.1)
          if t % 10 ==0:
             beta=max(1e-12, beta*beta_decay)
             # if current max much above best, increase beta? 
       else:
          z=best_z.copy(); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; continue
       if no > no_imp_limit:
          if noise_amp is None:
             na=0.08 if best_score < 1.505 else 0.2
          else: na=noise_amp
          nz=smooth_noise(n, rng, na, 48)
          z=best_z + nz
          z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=z.mean()
          p=softmax_from_z(z)
          beta=max(1e-8, beta_frac*(2.0*best_score/n))
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; current_lr=lr; focus=1.0; no=0
    return best,best_score
```
If n large, `1-b1**t` underflows? t up to 1e6? b1**t underflows to0, fine. Use if t small? okay.

Potential issue: `gz` all zero for uniform? Then Adam no update. But if score already lower? Fine.

```
def heat_opt(p0, deadline, rng, eta=0.05, beta_frac=0.05, focus=1.5, no_imp_limit=300):
    p=normalize; z=log; best; current=best; current_score; current_eta=eta; no=0
    while time<deadline:
        c=fft_convolve(p); maxc; score
        if score<best...

        w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus)
        hp=corr_w_p(w,p)
        mean=hp.mean(); std=hp.std()
        if std>1e-300:
           delta=hp-mean; delta/= (std if std>0 else 1)
        else: delta=0
        z = z - current_eta*delta
        z=clip; p=softmax
        new_score=score_seq(p)
        if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < current_score - 1e-16:
           current_score=new_score; current_eta=min(0.5,current_eta*1.05); no=0
        else:
           eta=max(0.005,current_eta*0.9); no+=1
           # revert? p is new; if no improve, maybe keep? Could drift. Revert to previous p? Keep previous p variable.
           p=prev_p.copy(); z=prev_z.copy(); current_score=current_score_prev
```

LP functions:
```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
   n=a.size
   nnz=n*(n+1)//2
   rows=np.empty(nnz,dtype=np.int32); cols=np.empty(nnz,dtype=np.int32); data=np.empty(nnz,dtype=np.float64)
   pos=0
   for d in range(n):
      cnt=n-d
      rows[pos:pos+cnt]=np.arange(d,d+cnt,dtype=np.int32)
      cols[pos:pos+cnt]=np.arange(cnt,dtype=np.int32)
      data[pos:pos+cnt]=a[d]
      pos+=cnt
   return csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
```
For n=2048, loop 2048, each vector assigns ~1024; okay. Could be 0.1s.

`lp_solve_prebuilt(A, b_ub, time_limit)`:
```
def lp_solve_prebuilt(A, b_ub, time_limit=60.0):
    n=A.shape[1]
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': max(1.0,float(time_limit))})
    except Exception:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
       x=np.maximum(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
       y=None
       if hasattr(res,'ineqlin') and res.ineqlin is not None:
          y=np.asarray(res.ineqlin,dtype=np.float64)
          y=np.where(np.isfinite(y),y,0.0)
          y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
       return x,y
    return None,None
```
If time_limit option not recognized, exception? It might be accepted. Good.

`lp_refine` as above. Need use `A` and try Ms.
```
def lp_refine(a0, deadline, max_solves=10, t_mix=0.35, shrink=0.998):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
   if a is None: return None, inf
   best=a.copy(); best_score=score_seq(a)
   n=a.size
   for sol in range(max_solves):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
      A=build_conv_matrix(a)
      c=fft_convolve(a); Mcur=float(c.max())
      if Mcur<=0: break
      b=None; y=None
      factors=[shrink, min(1.0, shrink+0.001), 1.0005]
      time_left=deadline-time.monotonic()-5
      per_try=max(5.0, time_left/3)
      for f in factors:
         if time.monotonic()>deadline-5: break
         b_ub=(Mcur*f)*np.ones(2*n-1)
         b,y=lp_solve_prebuilt(A,b_ub,per_try)
         if b is not None:
             if b.sum()>1e-8: break
      if b is None or b.sum()<=1e-8: break
      b=b/b.sum()
      a_new=(1-t_mix)*a + t_mix*b; a_new=normalize_nonneg(a_new)
      if a_new is None: break
      sc=score_seq(a_new)
      if not np.isfinite(sc): break
      if sc<best_score:
         best_score=sc; best=a_new.copy()
      # dual grad
      if y is not None:
         s_y=float(y.sum())
         if s_y>1e-12:
            yw=y/s_y
            hp=corr_w_p(yw,a_new)
            z=np.log(np.maximum(a_new,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
            std=hp.std()
            if std>0: z = z - 0.05*(hp-hp.mean())/std
            cand=softmax_from_z(z)
            scd=score_seq(cand)
            if np.isfinite(scd) and scd<sc:
                a_new=cand; sc=scd
                if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best=a_new.copy()
      if sc < best_score*1.02:
         a=a_new
      else:
         a=best.copy()
      c=fft_convolve(a); Mcur=float(c.max())
      shrink=0.999? keep
      Mcur=min(Mcur, best_score/(2*n)*1.01)
      t_mix=max(0.12, t_mix*0.92)
   return best,best_score
```

```
def quick_profiles(n=1024):
   cand=[]
   alphas=[0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2,1.5]
   for a in alphas:
      cand.append(profile_linear(n,a))
      cand.append(profile_linear(n,-a))
   for pwr in [0.3,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0]:
      cand.append(profile_power(n,pwr)); cand.append(profile_power(n,pwr)[::-1].copy())
   for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
      cand.append(profile_exp(n,lam)); cand.append(profile_exp(n,-lam))
   for mu in [0.2,0.8]:
      for sig in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3]:
         cand.append(profile_gauss(n,mu,sig)); cand.append(profile_gauss(n,1-mu,sig))
   return cand
```
This many (50) each FFT 1024 cheap ~0.01s? 50*0.001=0.05s. Good.

Let's write `heat_opt` carefully.

```
def heat_opt(p0, deadline, rng, eta=0.03, beta_frac=0.05, focus=1.5, no_imp_limit=400):
   p=normalize_nonneg(p0); if None return
   n=p.size
   z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
   c=fft_convolve(p); maxc; score
   best=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); best_score=score
   current_score=score; current_eta=eta; no=0
   prev_p=p.copy(); prev_z=z.copy(); prev_score=score
   while time<deadline:
      if prev_score<=0: break
      c=fft_convolve(p); maxc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*maxc)
      if score<best_score: best...
      if c.size==0 or maxc<=0: break
      beta=max(1e-12, beta_frac*maxc)
      w=soft_weights(c,beta,focus)
      hp=corr_w_p(w,p)
      mean=float(hp.mean()); std=float(hp.std())
      if std>1e-300: delta=(hp-mean)/std
      else: delta=np.zeros(n)
      z_new = prev_z - current_eta*delta
      z_new=clip; z_new-=mean
      cand=softmax_from_z(z_new)
      sc=score_seq(cand)
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < prev_score - 1e-16:
         prev_p=cand.copy(); prev_z=z_new.copy(); prev_score=sc; p=cand; z=z_new
         current_eta=min(0.5, current_eta*1.05); no=0
         if sc<best_score: best...
      else:
         p=prev_p; z=prev_z
         current_eta=max(0.005, current_eta*0.95); no+=1
         if no > no_imp_limit:
             na=0.3 if best_score<1.505 else 0.6
             nz=smooth_noise(n,rng,na,48)
             z=best_z+nz; z=clip; z-=mean
             p=softmax_from_z(z); prev_p=p.copy(); prev_z=z.copy()
             prev_score=score_seq(p); current_eta=eta; no=0
```

```
def profile_splits(n, r):
   w=[1.0]
   while len(w)<n:
      new=[]
      for val in w:
         new.append(val*r); new.append(val*(1-r))
      w=new
   w=w[:n]
   return np.array(w,dtype=float)/sum
```

```python
"""
Optimization for nonnegative step functions minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

Strategy:
- Normalize to sum 1 and work in log-softmax coordinates.
- Use smooth-max / soft-hinge gradient information for the convolution peak.
- Interpolated/coarsen-scaled multi-scale refinement (1024 -> 2048 -> 4096 -> 8192).
- Periodic LP refinement using the state-of-the-art linear program
  (max sum b subject to a*b <= M) plus its dual variables.
- Smooth multiplicative heat moves for large-scale late-stage polishing.
- Final exact validation through evaluate_sequence when feasible.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix


def next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def fft_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def score_normalized(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_convolve(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    maxc = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * p.size * maxc)


def score_seq(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    maxc = float(c.max())
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * maxc / (s * s))


def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return None
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(z)
    e = e + 1e-300
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def soft_weights(c, beta, focus=1.0, baseline=0.0):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        m = 0.0
        c = np.zeros_like(c)
    if beta <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = c >= m - 1e-12
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt == 0:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w[mask] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
    else:
        x = (c - m) / beta
        ex = np.exp(x)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ex / s
            if focus != 1.0:
                f = float(focus)
                if f > 1.0:
                    w = np.power(np.maximum(w, 1e-300), f)
                    sw = float(w.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(sw) and sw > 0.0:
                        w /= sw
                    else:
                        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
                        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    if baseline is not None and float(baseline) > 0.0:
        b = float(np.clip(baseline, 0.0, 0.5))
        uniform = np.ones_like(w) / float(w.size)
        w = (1.0 - b) * w + b * uniform
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if np.isfinite(ws) and ws > 0.0:
            w /= ws
    return w


def corr_w_p(w, p):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size < need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:w.size] = w
        w = tmp
    elif w.size > need:
        w = w[:need]
    N = next_pow2(need)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, N)[n - 1: n + n - 1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h


def smooth_noise(n, rng, amp=0.2, max_freq=48):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = min(max_freq, n // 2)
    for k in range(1, m + 1):
        a = float(amp) / float(k)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        noise += a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x / float(n) + phase)
    noise -= float(noise.mean())
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(noise))) if noise.size else 0.0
    if np.isfinite(mx) and mx > 0.0:
        noise *= (float(amp) / mx)
    return noise


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, p, s):
    if p is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    if s is None:
        s = score_seq(p)
    if np.isfinite(s) and float(s) < float(best_score):
        return np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(s)
    return best_seq, best_score


def update_active(active, active_score, p, s):
    if p is None:
        return active, active_score
    if s is None:
        s = score_seq(p)
    if np.isfinite(s) and (active is None or float(s) < float(active_score)):
        return np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(s)
    return active, active_score


def interp_profile(p0, n_new):
    p0 = normalize_nonneg(p0)
    if p0 is None:
        return None
    n0 = p0.size
    if n0 == n_new:
        return p0.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    p = np.interp(x1, x0, p0)
    return normalize_nonneg(p)


def repeat_profile(p0, n_new):
    p0 = normalize_nonneg(p0)
    if p0 is None:
        return None
    n0 = p0.size
    if n0 == n_new:
        return p0.copy()
    rep = int(np.ceil(float(n_new) / float(n0)))
    p = np.repeat(p0, rep)[:n_new]
    return normalize_nonneg(p)


def make_best_interp(bases, n_target):
    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf
    for b in bases:
        if b is None:
            continue
        for mode in range(2):
            if b.size == n_target:
                p = b.copy()
            else:
                if mode == 0:
                    p = interp_profile(b, n_target)
                else:
                    p = repeat_profile(b, n_target)
            if p is None:
                continue
            s = score_seq(p)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_s = float(s)
    return best_p, best_s


def profile_linear(n, alpha):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + float(alpha) * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def profile_power(n, p):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if abs(p) < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = np.power(x + 1e-12, float(p))
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def profile_exp(n, lam):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.exp(float(lam) * (x - 0.5))
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def profile_gauss(n, mu, sigma):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    s2 = max(1e-8, float(sigma) * float(sigma))
    a = np.exp(-((x - float(mu)) ** 2) / (2.0 * s2))
    a = a + 1e-12
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def profile_splits(n, r):
    w = [1.0]
    while len(w) < n:
        nw = []
        for v in w:
            nw.append(v * float(r))
            nw.append(v * (1.0 - float(r)))
        w = nw
    w = np.asarray(w[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_nonneg(w)


def quick_profiles(n):
    out = []
    for a in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.2):
        out.append(profile_linear(n, a))
        out.append(profile_linear(n, -a))
    for pw in (0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        out.append(profile_power(n, pw))
        out.append(profile_power(n, pw)[::-1].copy())
    for lam in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        out.append(profile_exp(n, lam))
        out.append(profile_exp(n, -lam))
    for mu in (0.2, 0.8):
        for sig in (0.08, 0.15, 0.25):
            out.append(profile_gauss(n, mu, sig))
            out.append(profile_gauss(n, 1.0 - mu, sig))
    for r in (0.65, 0.75, 0.85):
        out.append(profile_splits(n, r))
        out.append(profile_splits(n, 1.0 - r))
    return out


def adam_opt(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, beta_frac=0.08, beta_decay=0.95,
             no_imp_limit=250, focus_start=1.0, baseline=0.03):
    p = normalize_nonneg(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(z.mean())

    b1, b2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    c = fft_convolve(p)
    maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    beta = max(1e-12, float(beta_frac) * maxc)

    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    best = p.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()

    no = 0
    focus = float(focus_start)
    current_lr = float(lr)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fft_convolve(p)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        w = soft_weights(c, beta, focus, baseline)
        gp = corr_w_p(w, p)
        dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
        gz = p * (gp - dot)
        gz -= float(gz.mean())

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_lr = float(lr)
            continue

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gz
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gz * gz)
        mh = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        z = z - current_lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_lr = float(lr)
            continue

        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
        z -= float(z.mean())
        p = softmax_from_z(z)
        if p is None:
            z = best_z.copy()
            p = softmax_from_z(best_z)
            continue

        c = fft_convolve(p)
        maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score = score
                best = p.copy()
                best_z = z.copy()
                no = 0
                focus = max(float(focus_start), focus * 0.85)
                current_lr = min(0.2, current_lr * 1.02)
            else:
                no += 1
                if no % 30 == 0:
                    current_lr = max(0.002, current_lr * 0.97)
                if no % 100 == 0:
                    focus = min(4.0, focus * 1.1)

            if t % 10 == 0:
                beta = max(1e-4 * maxc, beta * float(beta_decay))
        else:
            z = best_z.copy()
            p = softmax_from_z(best_z)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_lr = float(lr)
            continue

        if no > int(no_imp_limit):
            if best_score < 1.505:
                amp = 0.08
            elif best_score < 1.53:
                amp = 0.18
            else:
                amp = 0.35
            nz = smooth_noise(n, rng, amp, 48)
            z = best_z + nz
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
            z -= float(z.mean())
            p = softmax_from_z(z)
            beta = max(1e-8, float(beta_frac) * (2.0 * best_score / float(n)))
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            current_lr = float(lr)
            focus = float(focus_start)
            no = 0

    return best, best_score


def heat_opt(p0, deadline, rng, eta=0.03, beta_frac=0.05, focus=1.5,
             baseline=0.06, no_imp_limit=350):
    p = normalize_nonneg(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(z.mean())

    c = fft_convolve(p)
    maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    best = p.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()

    prev_p = p.copy()
    prev_z = z.copy()
    prev_score = best_score
    current_eta = float(eta)
    no = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if prev_score <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(prev_score):
            break
        c = fft_convolve(prev_p)
        maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = prev_p.copy()
            best_z = prev_z.copy()
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        beta = max(1e-12, float(beta_frac) * maxc)
        w = soft_weights(c, beta, focus, baseline)
        hp = corr_w_p(w, prev_p)
        mean = float(hp.mean())
        std = float(hp.std())
        if std > 1e-300:
            delta = (hp - mean) / std
        else:
            delta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        z_new = prev_z - current_eta * delta
        z_new = np.clip(z_new, -50.0, 50.0)
        z_new -= float(z_new.mean())
        cand = softmax_from_z(z_new)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = score_seq(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < prev_score - 1e-16:
            prev_p = cand.copy()
            prev_z = z_new.copy()
            prev_score = float(sc)
            current_eta = min(0.5, current_eta * 1.05)
            no = 0
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best = prev_p.copy()
                best_z = prev_z.copy()
        else:
            current_eta = max(0.005, current_eta * 0.95)
            no += 1
            if no > int(no_imp_limit):
                amp = 0.35 if best_score < 1.505 else 0.65
                nz = smooth_noise(n, rng, amp, 48)
                z_new = best_z + nz
                z_new = np.clip(z_new, -50.0, 50.0)
                z_new -= float(z_new.mean())
                prev_p = softmax_from_z(z_new)
                prev_z = z_new.copy()
                prev_score = score_seq(prev_p)
                current_eta = float(eta)
                no = 0

    return best, best_score


def build_conv_rows_cols(n):
    nnz = n * (n + 1) // 2
    rows = np.empty(nnz, dtype=np.int32)
    cols = np.empty(nnz, dtype=np.int32)
    pos = 0
    for d in range(n):
        cnt = n - d
        rows[pos:pos + cnt] = np.arange(d, d + cnt, dtype=np.int32)
        cols[pos:pos + cnt] = np.arange(cnt, dtype=np.int32)
        pos += cnt
    return rows, cols


def build_conv_matrix(a, rows=None, cols=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    nnz_expected = n * (n + 1) // 2
    if rows is None or cols is None or rows.size != nnz_expected or cols.size != nnz_expected:
        rows, cols = build_conv_rows_cols(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    a = a / a.sum()
    data = np.empty(rows.size, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = 0
    for d in range(n):
        cnt = n - d
        data[pos:pos + cnt] = a[d]
        pos += cnt
    return csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))


def lp_solve_prebuilt(A, b_ub, time_limit=30.0):
    n = A.shape[1]
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.asarray(b_ub, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method='highs',
            options={'time_limit': max(1.0, float(time_limit))}
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.asarray(b_ub, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=bounds,
                method='highs'
            )
        except Exception:
            return None, None

    if res is None:
        return None, None
    if not getattr(res, 'success', False):
        return None, None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)

    y = None
    if hasattr(res, 'ineqlin') and res.ineqlin is not None:
        y = np.asarray(res.ineqlin, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return x, y


def lp_refine(a0, deadline, max_solves=10, t_mix=0.35, shrink=0.997):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_seq(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    rows, cols = build_conv_rows_cols(n)
    current_t = float(t_mix)
    current_shrink = float(shrink)

    for _ in range(int(max_solves)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

        A = build_conv_matrix(a, rows, cols)
        c = fft_convolve(a)
        Mcur = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(Mcur) or Mcur <= 0.0:
            break

        time_left = max(5.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0))
        per_try = min(40.0, max(5.0, time_left / 3.0))

        factors = [1.0, current_shrink, max(0.95, current_shrink * 0.99)]
        b = None
        y = None

        for f in factors:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            b_ub = (Mcur * float(f)) * np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            bb, yy = lp_solve_prebuilt(A, b_ub, per_try)
            if bb is None:
                continue
            sbb = float(bb.sum())
            if sbb < 1e-8:
                continue
            if f >= 1.0 and np.linalg.norm(bb / sbb - a, ord=1) < 1e-3:
                continue
            b = bb / sbb
            y = yy
            break

        if b is None:
            break

        a_new = (1.0 - current_t) * a + current_t * b
        a_new = normalize_nonng_norm = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
        if a_new is None:
            break

        sc = score_seq(a_new)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        # Optional dual-guided polish using LP inequality multipliers.
        if y is not None:
            sy = float(np.sum(y))
            if np.isfinite(sy) and sy > 1e-12:
                yw = y / sy
                hp = corr_w_p(yw, a_new)
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300))
                z -= float(z.mean())
                mean = float(hp.mean())
                std = float(hp.std())
                if std > 1e-300:
                    z = z - 0.05 * (hp - mean) / std
                cand = softmax_from_z(z)
                if cand is not None:
                    scd = score_seq(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(scd) and scd < sc:
                        a_new = cand
                        sc = scd

        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best = a_new.copy()

        if sc < best_score * 1.02 + 1e-12:
            a = a_new
            current_shrink = max(0.985, current_shrink * 0.995)
        else:
            a = best.copy()
            current_shrink = max(0.985, current_shrink * 0.98)

        current_t = max(0.12, current_t * 0.93)

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 90.0)
    except Exception:
        total = 1710.0
    search_deadline = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    active_seq = None
    active_score = np.inf
    parent = None

    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
        par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(par.sum())
        if par.size > 0 and np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.01:
            parent = par / s
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = profile_linear(1024, 0.8)

    ps = score_seq(parent)
    if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_score:
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        best_score = float(ps)
        active_seq = parent.copy()
        active_score = float(ps)
    print(f"START parent_len={0 if parent is None else parent.size} score={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Phase boundaries.
    span = max(60.0, search_deadline - t0)
    end1 = t0 + min(220.0, 0.15 * span)
    end2 = t0 + min(680.0, 0.40 * span)
    end3 = t0 + min(1180.0, 0.72 * span)
    end4 = search_deadline - 30.0

    # Phase 1: quick local polish of parent and optional profile exploration.
    if time.monotonic() < end1 - 60.0 and active_seq is not None and active_seq.size <= 2048:
        per = min(end1 - time.monotonic() - 60.0, 90.0)
        if per > 10.0:
            p1, s1 = adam_opt(active_seq, time.monotonic() + per, rng,
                              lr=0.06, beta_frac=0.08, no_imp_limit=200)
            active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p1, s1)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p1, s1)
            print(f"PHASE1 adam len={0 if p1 is None else p1.size} score={s1:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    if best_score > 1.55 and time.monotonic() < end1 - 70.0:
        prof_best = None
        prof_score = np.inf
        for pp in quick_profiles(1024):
            if pp is None:
                continue
            ss = score_seq(pp)
            if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < prof_score:
                prof_best = pp
                prof_score = float(ss)
        if prof_best is not None and prof_score < min(1.75, best_score + 0.3):
            per = min(end1 - time.monotonic() - 70.0, 50.0)
            if per > 5.0:
                p_p, s_p = adam_opt(prof_best, time.monotonic() + per, rng,
                                    lr=0.08, beta_frac=0.09, no_imp_limit=120)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p_p, s_p)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_p, s_p)
                print(f"PHASE1 profile len={0 if p_p is None else p_p.size} score={s_p:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < end1 - 10.0 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 2048:
        p_lp, s_lp = lp_refine(best_seq, end1 - 5.0,
                               max_solves=10 if best_seq.size <= 1024 else 4)
        active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p_lp, s_lp)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_lp, s_lp)
        print(f"PHASE1 lp len={0 if p_lp is None else p_lp.size} score={s_lp:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 2: n=2048 refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < end2:
        p_int, s_int = make_best_interp([best_seq, active_seq], 2048)
        if p_int is not None:
            active_seq = p_int.copy()
            active_score = float(s_int)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, active_seq, active_score)
            print(f"PHASE2 init2048 score={active_score:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            per = min(end2 - time.monotonic() - 120.0, 350.0)
            if per > 20.0:
                p2, s2 = adam_opt(active_seq, time.monotonic() + per, rng,
                                  lr=0.045, beta_frac=0.07, no_imp_limit=300)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p2, s2)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p2, s2)
                print(f"PHASE2 adam2048 score={s2:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < end2 - 50.0 and active_seq is not None and active_seq.size <= 2048:
                p2a, s2a = lp_refine(active_seq, end2 - 10.0, max_solves=4, t_mix=0.30, shrink=0.997)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p2a, s2a)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p2a, s2a)
                print(f"PHASE2 lp2048 score={s2a:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < end2 - 10.0 and active_seq is not None:
                p2b, s2b = heat_opt(active_seq, end2 - 5.0, rng, eta=0.03, focus=1.5)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p2b, s2b)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p2b, s2b)
                print(f"PHASE2 heat2048 score={s2b:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 3: n=4096 refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < end3:
        p_int, s_int = make_best_interp([best_seq, active_seq], 4096)
        if p_int is not None:
            active_seq = p_int.copy()
            active_score = float(s_int)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, active_seq, active_score)
            print(f"PHASE3 init4096 score={active_score:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            per = min(end3 - time.monotonic() - 70.0, 500.0)
            if per > 30.0:
                p3, s3 = adam_opt(active_seq, time.monotonic() + per, rng,
                                  lr=0.032, beta_frac=0.06, no_imp_limit=350)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p3, s3)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p3, s3)
                print(f"PHASE3 adam4096 score={s3:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < end3 - 10.0 and active_seq is not None:
                p3a, s3a = heat_opt(active_seq, end3 - 5.0, rng, eta=0.025, focus=1.4)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p3a, s3a)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p3a, s3a)
                print(f"PHASE3 heat4096 score={s3a:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 4: n=8192 refinement, if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < end4:
        p_int, s_int = make_best_interp([best_seq, active_seq], 8192)
        if p_int is not None:
            active_seq = p_int.copy()
            active_score = float(s_int)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, active_seq, active_score)
            print(f"PHASE4 init8192 score={active_score:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            per = min(end4 - time.monotonic() - 30.0, 350.0)
            if per > 30.0:
                p4, s4 = adam_opt(active_seq, time.monotonic() + per, rng,
                                  lr=0.022, beta_frac=0.05, no_imp_limit=350)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p4, s4)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p4, s4)
                print(f"PHASE4 adam8192 score={s4:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < end4 - 5.0 and active_seq is not None:
                p4a, s4a = heat_opt(active_seq, end4 - 5.0, rng, eta=0.018, focus=1.4)
                active_seq, active_score = update_active(active_seq, active_score, p4a, s4a)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p4a, s4a)
                print(f"PHASE4 heat8192 score={s4a:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Final short polish.
    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 10.0 and best_seq is not None:
        if best_seq.size <= 2048:
            pf, sf = lp_refine(best_seq, search_deadline - 20.0, max_solves=2, t_mix=0.20, shrink=0.999)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, pf, sf)
            print(f"FINAL lp score={sf:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

        if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 5.0:
            pf, sf = heat_opt(best_seq, search_deadline - 5.0, rng, eta=0.018, focus=1.35, no_imp_limit=80)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, pf, sf)
            print(f"FINAL heat score={sf:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Select best final candidate by exact evaluator when possible.
    candidates = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        candidates.append(best_seq)
    if parent is not None and (not candidates or parent.size not in [c.size for c in candidates]):
        candidates.append(parent)
    if active_seq is not None and active_seq.size not in [c.size for c in candidates]:
        candidates.append(active_seq)

    final_arr = None
    final_score = best_score
    for cand in candidates:
        arr = normalize_nonneg(cand)
        if arr is None:
            continue
        local = score_seq(arr)
        ev = np.inf
        if arr.size <= 8192:
            try:
                ev = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr]))
            except Exception:
                ev = np.inf
        actual = float(ev) if np.isfinite(ev) else local
        if np.isfinite(actual) and actual < final_score:
            final_score = actual
            final_arr = arr.copy()

    if final_arr is None:
        final_arr = normalize_nonneg(best_seq)
    if final_arr is None:
        final_arr = profile_linear(1024, 0.8)

    final_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(final_arr), final_arr, 0.0)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    sfin = float(final_arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sfin) or sfin <= 0.01:
        final_arr = profile_linear(1024, 0.8)
        sfin = float(final_arr.sum())
    final_arr = final_arr / sfin

    print(f"RETURN len={final_arr.size} local={score_seq(final_arr):.10f}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final_arr]
```
